province_event = {
	id = 24001
	title = "EVTNAME24001"
	desc = "EVTDESC24001"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		NOT = {
			is_core = FRA
		}
		culture = french
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = FRA
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = FRA
			}
			civilized = yes
			NOT = {
				any_owned_province = {
					is_core = FRA
				}
			}
			FRA = {
				OR = {
					accepted_culture = french
					primary_culture = french
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24001"
		add_core = FRA
	}
}

	desc = "EVTDESC24005"

		}

		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = D26
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = D26
			}
			NOT = {
				any_owned_province = {
					is_core = D26
				}
			}
			D26 = {
			}
		}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24005"
		add_core = D26
		owner = {
			D26 = {
				add_accepted_culture = north_german
			}
		}
	}

	id = 24030
	title = "EVTNAME24030"
	desc = "EVTDESC24030"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		NOT = {
			is_core = SCH
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			is_core = SCH
		}
		any_pop = {
			has_pop_culture = north_german

	title = "EVTNAME24290"
	desc = "EVTDESC24290"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		NOT = {
			is_core = UKR
		}
		culture = ukrainian
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = UKR
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = UKR
			}
			civilized = yes
			NOT = {
				any_owned_province = {
					is_core = UKR
				}
			}
			UKR = {
				OR = {
					accepted_culture = ukrainian
					primary_culture = ukrainian
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24290"
		add_core = UKR
	}
}
province_event = {
	id = 24291
	title = "EVTNAME24291"
	desc = "EVTDESC24291"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		NOT = {
			is_core = UKR
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			is_core = UKR
		}
		any_pop = {
			has_pop_culture = ukrainian
		}
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = UKR
			}
			UKR = {
				OR = {
					accepted_culture = ukrainian

				}
			}

	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}

		add_core = UKR

}

	title = "EVTNAME24521"
	desc = "EVTDESC24521"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		NOT = {
			is_core = D12
		}
		culture = shan
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = D12
			}
			NOT = {
				exists = D12
			}
			NOT = {
				any_owned_province = {
					is_core = D12
				}
			}
			D12 = {
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24521"
		add_core = D12
		owner = {
			D12 = {
				add_accepted_culture = shan
			}
		}
	}
}
province_event = {
	id = 24522
	title = "EVTNAME24522"
	desc = "EVTDESC24522"
	trigger = {
		controlled_by_rebels = yes
		NOT = {
			is_core = D12
		}
		any_neighbor_province = {
			is_core = D12
		}
		any_pop = {
			has_pop_culture = shan
		}
		owner = {
			NOT = {
				tag = D12
			}
			D12 = {
				OR = {
					accepted_culture = shan
					primary_culture = shan
				}
			}
		}
	}
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		months = 4
	}
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTA24522"
		add_core = D12
	}
}
